### [PASSED] `src/tests/tools/evaluation/test_ranking_streaming.py::test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality[candidates]`  (2.4013s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interrupt_stage", ["candidates", "scored:"])
def test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality(environment,monkeypatch,interrupt_stage):
    base,paths,items = environment
    reference = launch(monkeypatch,base,"reference")
    original = RankingRunStore.append
    def interrupt(self,stream,batch):
        original(self,stream,batch)
        if stream.startswith(interrupt_stage) and self.count(stream)==1:
            raise RuntimeError("simulated power loss after commit")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"append",interrupt)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="power loss"):
            launch(patch,base,"resumed")
    result = launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    for name in ("candidate_lists.jsonl","scored.jsonl"):
        a=[json.loads(l) for l in (reference/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        b=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        for rows in (a,b):
            for row in rows:
                for field in ("source_latency_seconds","ranking_latency_seconds","fetch_latency_seconds"):
                    row.pop(field,None)
        assert a==b
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert sum(v["semantic_errors"] for v in m["tool_health"].values())==0
    # The stream summary reproduces existing batch metric implementations.
    rows=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            selected=[r for r in rows if r["merge"]==merge and r["segment"]==segment and r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]]
            old=script._ranking_metrics([[items[i] for i in r["ranked_ids"]] for r in selected],
                                       [set(r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]) for r in selected],[1,3],len(items))
            assert old["metrics"]==m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]["metrics"]
    # A finished checkpoint is idempotent and does not publish exp_002.
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    assert not (result.parent/"exp_002").exists()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/tools/evaluation/test_ranking_streaming.py::test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality[scored:]`  (2.1189s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interrupt_stage", ["candidates", "scored:"])
def test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality(environment,monkeypatch,interrupt_stage):
    base,paths,items = environment
    reference = launch(monkeypatch,base,"reference")
    original = RankingRunStore.append
    def interrupt(self,stream,batch):
        original(self,stream,batch)
        if stream.startswith(interrupt_stage) and self.count(stream)==1:
            raise RuntimeError("simulated power loss after commit")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"append",interrupt)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="power loss"):
            launch(patch,base,"resumed")
    result = launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    for name in ("candidate_lists.jsonl","scored.jsonl"):
        a=[json.loads(l) for l in (reference/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        b=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        for rows in (a,b):
            for row in rows:
                for field in ("source_latency_seconds","ranking_latency_seconds","fetch_latency_seconds"):
                    row.pop(field,None)
        assert a==b
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert sum(v["semantic_errors"] for v in m["tool_health"].values())==0
    # The stream summary reproduces existing batch metric implementations.
    rows=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            selected=[r for r in rows if r["merge"]==merge and r["segment"]==segment and r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]]
            old=script._ranking_metrics([[items[i] for i in r["ranked_ids"]] for r in selected],
                                       [set(r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]) for r in selected],[1,3],len(items))
            assert old["metrics"]==m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]["metrics"]
    # A finished checkpoint is idempotent and does not publish exp_002.
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    assert not (result.parent/"exp_002").exists()
```

```python
def test_frozen_candidates_and_weight_variants(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    source = launch(monkeypatch,base,"source")
    def no_retrieval(*a,**k):
        raise AssertionError("frozen mode must not call SemanticSearch")
    monkeypatch.setattr(script.SearchTool,"execute",no_retrieval)
    result=launch(monkeypatch,base,"frozen",frozen=source,weights="0,1e-6")
    assert (source/"candidate_lists.jsonl").read_bytes()==(result/"candidate_lists.jsonl").read_bytes()
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert set(m["ranking"]["union_rr"])=={"baseline","popularity_weight_0"}
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            a=json.loads((source/"metrics.json").read_text())["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]
            b=m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]
            assert a["metrics"]==b["metrics"]
```

```python
    base,paths,items = environment
    export = RankingRunStore.export
    def fail(self,streams,destination):
        export(self,streams,destination)
        if Path(destination).name == "scored.jsonl":
            raise RuntimeError("export interrupted")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"export",fail)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="export interrupted"):
            launch(patch,base,"export_resume")
    assert not (base/"export_resume_output"/"exp_001").exists()
    def no_models(*a,**k):
        raise AssertionError("completed scoring must not rebuild models on resume")
    monkeypatch.setattr(script,"_run_stages",no_models)
    result=launch(monkeypatch,base,"export_resume",resume=True)
    assert len((result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines())==6
```

```python
        return original(*a,**k)
```

```python
def test_single_run_never_gives_a_verdict_and_warns():
    ra, rb = _runs(1, 40, "similar", lambda i, r: 0.0, lambda i, r: 1.0)
    g = analyze_metric(ra, rb, A, B, "task_success")["realistic/similar"]
    assert g["verdict"] == "inconclusive"
    assert any("single run" in w for w in g["warnings"])
    assert g["run_std_baseline"] is None
```

```python
def test_oracle_and_realistic_are_separate_groups_and_no_overall_group():
    ra1, rb1 = _runs(2, 25, "similar", lambda i, r: 0.0, lambda i, r: 1.0)
    ra2, rb2 = _runs(2, 25, "constrained", lambda i, r: 1.0, lambda i, r: 0.0)
    ra = [x + y for x, y in zip(ra1, ra2)]
    rb = [x + y for x, y in zip(rb1, rb2)]
    res = analyze_metric(ra, rb, A, B, "task_success")
    assert res["realistic"]["diff"] == 1.0 and res["oracle"]["diff"] == -1.0
    assert not any(k in res for k in ("all", "overall", "total"))
    assert any("ORACLE" in w for w in res["oracle"]["warnings"])
```

```python
def test_unscored_baseline_is_counted_not_dropped():

    g = analyze_metric(ra, rb, A, B, "task_success")["realistic/personalized"]
```

```python
def test_cli_end_to_end(tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
    dirs = []
    for run in range(3):
        d = tmp_path / f"exp_{run}"
        d.mkdir()
        rows = (_rows(A, "similar", "warm", 25, lambda i, r: float(i % 2 == 0), run)
                + _rows(B, "similar", "warm", 25, lambda i, r: 1.0, run)
                + _rows(B, "constrained", "warm", 25, lambda i, r: 1.0, run))
        (d / "scored.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(r) for r in rows), encoding="utf-8")
        dirs.append(str(d))
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    # resamples/output dir đi qua CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH (configs/eval/memory_utility.yaml + data_paths.outputs)
    overlay = tmp_path / "overlay.yaml"
    overlay.write_text(yaml.safe_dump({
        "eval/memory_utility": {"memory_utility": {"resamples": 500}},
        "path/data_paths": {"outputs": {"memory_utility": str(out)}},
    }), encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay))
    assert main(["--dirs", *dirs]) == 0
    data = json.loads((out / "memory_utility.json").read_text())
    assert data["meta"]["runs_a"] == 3
    assert data["result"]["task_success"]["realistic/similar"]["verdict"] == "memory_better"
    assert data["result"]["task_success"]["oracle/constrained"]["verdict"] == "no_pairs"
    assert (out / "memory_utility.md").exists()
```

```python
    bare = _trajectory(steps=[_step("SimilarTool"), _step("RankTool")], recommended=[VG_A, VG_B])
    row_bare = score_trajectory(_request(), bare, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)

    assert row_full["task_success"] is True and row_full["recall@2"] == row_bare["recall@2"]
```

```python
def test_a_fallback_is_counted_and_grounded_is_only_defined_for_ok_answers(corpus):
    fell_back = score_trajectory(_request(), _answered(fallback="ungrounded: x", composer="template"), item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2)
```

```python
def test_summarize_averages_the_answer_columns_and_skips_missing():

    rows = [
        {**base, "request_id": "a", "answer_grounded": True, "answer_fallback": False, "answer_status_ok": True, "answer_status_tool_error": False},
        {**base, "request_id": "b", "answer_grounded": True, "answer_fallback": True, "answer_status_ok": True, "answer_status_tool_error": False},
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_eval_integration.py::test_run_planner_adds_the_answer_without_touching_the_recommendation_or_planner_usage`  (0.0172s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_run_planner_adds_the_answer_without_touching_the_recommendation_or_planner_usage(corpus):
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    plain = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), _two_items_request(), "planner", 2)

    def answerer(outcome):
        infer_llm = ScriptedLLM(good_reply(outcome.items))
        return compose_answer(outcome, infer_llm, mode="llm")

    with_answer = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), _two_items_request(), "planner", 2, answerer=answerer, inference_mode="llm")
    for key in ("recommended", "plan", "error", "reflections", "first_attempt_ok", "ask_user"):
        assert with_answer[key] == plain[key], key
    brief = lambda t: [(s["tool"], s["ok"], s["error"]) for s in t["steps"]]  # latency differs between two runs
    assert brief(with_answer) == brief(plain)
    assert {k: v for k, v in with_answer["usage"].items() if k != "latency_seconds"} == {k: v for k, v in plain["usage"].items() if k != "latency_seconds"}
    assert with_answer["recommended"] == [VG_A, VG_B]
    assert with_answer["answer_status"] == "ok" and with_answer["answer_composer"] == "llm" and with_answer["answer_grounded"] is True
    assert with_answer["answer_fallback"] is None and VG_A in with_answer["answer"] and with_answer["inference_mode"] == "llm"
    assert with_answer["inference_usage"]["attempts"] == 1 and with_answer["usage"]["attempts"] == 1  # two separate ledgers
    json.dumps(with_answer, ensure_ascii=False)
```

```python
def test_run_type1_keeps_the_recommendation_and_adds_the_answer():
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="similar-warm-0", kind="similar", segment="warm", text="similar to x", user_id="u1", seed_item_id=VG_A, targets=[1])
    out = {"ok": True, "cold_start": False, "items": [{"rank": 1, "item_id": VG_B, "score": 0.4, "domain": "Video_Games", "title": "Beta", "price": 3.0}]}
    traj = script.run_type1(_FakeType1(out), request, 2, answerer=lambda o: compose_answer(o, None, mode="template"), inference_mode="template")
    assert traj["agent"] == "type1" and traj["recommended"] == [VG_B] and traj["error"] is None and traj["usage"] == {}
    assert traj["answer_status"] == "ok" and traj["answer_composer"] == "template" and traj["answer_fallback"] is None and VG_B in traj["answer"]
    failed = script.run_type1(_FakeType1({"ok": False, "stage": "rank", "error": "boom"}), request, 2,
                              answerer=lambda o: compose_answer(o, None, mode="template"), inference_mode="template")
    assert failed["error"] == "rank: boom" and failed["recommended"] == [] and failed["answer_status"] == "tool_error"
    bare = script.run_type1(_FakeType1(out), request, 2)
    assert bare["recommended"] == [VG_B] and bare["answer"] is None
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/agent/test_eval_integration.py::test_resume_keeps_old_records_and_drops_other_inference_modes`  (0.003s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_keeps_old_records_and_drops_other_inference_modes():
    keep = _load_run_agent_eval().is_resumable
    text_of = {"r0": "hello"}
    rec = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "planner", "text": "hello", "reflect_rounds": 0, "inference_mode": "llm"}
    assert keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "llm")
    assert not keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "template")  # LLM and template answers must not be mixed in one experiment
    old = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != "inference_mode"}  # written before Inference existed: reusable, answer columns stay empty
    assert keep(old, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "llm") and keep(old, text_of, ["planner"], 0, "template")
    type1 = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "type1", "text": "hello", "inference_mode": "llm"}
    assert keep(type1, text_of, ["type1"], 0, "llm") and not keep(type1, text_of, ["type1"], 0, "template")
    baseline = {"request_id": "r0", "agent": "baseline", "text": "hello", "inference_mode": None}
    assert keep(baseline, text_of, ["baseline"], 0, "template")  # no Inference for the baseline
    assert keep(rec, text_of, ["planner"], 0)  # the old call signature still works
```

_inference.mode sống trong configs/agent/inference.yaml (hoặc CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH), không còn là CLI flag._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_inference_mode_comes_from_yaml_not_cli(monkeypatch, capsys, tmp_path):
    """inference.mode sống trong configs/agent/inference.yaml (hoặc CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH), không còn là CLI flag."""
    from package.config.load_config import ConfigError, load_config

    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", ["run_agent_eval.py", "--help"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as helped:
        script.main()
    assert helped.value.code == 0 and "--inference-mode" not in capsys.readouterr().out

    overlay = tmp_path / "overlay.yaml"
    overlay.write_text("agent/inference:\n  inference:\n    mode: gpt\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay))
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        load_config("agent/inference")
    monkeypatch.delenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH")
    assert load_config("agent/inference")["inference"]["mode"] in ("llm", "template")
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["llm", "template"])
def test_end_to_end_scenario(corpus, mode):
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="similar-warm-0", kind="similar", segment="warm", text=f"similar to {VG_A} and {VG_B}", targets=[1])
    from tests.agents.inference.helpers import item, good_reply

    test_items = [item(VG_A, "Game A"), item(VG_B, "Game B")]
    infer_llm = ScriptedLLM(good_reply(test_items) if mode == "llm" else "")

    def answerer(outcome):
        return compose_answer(outcome, infer_llm, mode=mode)

    traj = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), request, "planner", 2, answerer=answerer, inference_mode=mode)
    baseline = script.empty_trajectory(request, "baseline", [])
    baseline["recommended"] = [VG_A]
    rows = [score_trajectory(request, t, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2) for t in (traj, baseline)]
    summary = summarize(rows)

    planner = summary["planner"]["similar"]["all"]
    assert planner["task_success"] == 1.0 and planner["answer_status_ok"] == 1.0 and planner["answer_grounded"] == 1.0
    assert planner["answer_fallback"] == 0.0  # mode=template is a choice, not a fallback
    assert len(infer_llm.calls) == (1 if mode == "llm" else 0)
    assert planner["inference_attempts"] == (1 if mode == "llm" else 0)
    assert "answer_grounded" not in summary["baseline"]["similar"]["all"]  # the baseline has no Inference
    json.dumps(summary)
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["llm", "template"])
def test_end_to_end_scenario(corpus, mode):
    script = _load_run_agent_eval()
    request = AgentRequest(request_id="similar-warm-0", kind="similar", segment="warm", text=f"similar to {VG_A} and {VG_B}", targets=[1])
    from tests.agents.inference.helpers import item, good_reply

    test_items = [item(VG_A, "Game A"), item(VG_B, "Game B")]
    infer_llm = ScriptedLLM(good_reply(test_items) if mode == "llm" else "")

    def answerer(outcome):
        return compose_answer(outcome, infer_llm, mode=mode)

    traj = script.run_planner(_executor(corpus, QUERY_PLAN), request, "planner", 2, answerer=answerer, inference_mode=mode)
    baseline = script.empty_trajectory(request, "baseline", [])
    baseline["recommended"] = [VG_A]
    rows = [score_trajectory(request, t, item2id=ITEM2ID, corpus=corpus, k=2) for t in (traj, baseline)]
    summary = summarize(rows)

    planner = summary["planner"]["similar"]["all"]
    assert planner["task_success"] == 1.0 and planner["answer_status_ok"] == 1.0 and planner["answer_grounded"] == 1.0
    assert planner["answer_fallback"] == 0.0  # mode=template is a choice, not a fallback
    assert len(infer_llm.calls) == (1 if mode == "llm" else 0)
    assert planner["inference_attempts"] == (1 if mode == "llm" else 0)
    assert "answer_grounded" not in summary["baseline"]["similar"]["all"]  # the baseline has no Inference
    json.dumps(summary)
```

```python
def test_data_config_has_domains_and_resource_paths():
    domains_config = load_config("path/domains")
    run_tag_config = load_config("path/run_tag")
    data_paths_config = load_config("path/data_paths")
    cleaning_config = load_config("data/cleaning")
```

```python
def test_env_interpolation_default_and_shell_semantics(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    (tmp_path / "demo.yaml").write_text("a: ${DEMO_KEY:-}\nb: ${DEMO_KEY:-fallback}\nc: x-${DEMO_KEY:-y}-z\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.delenv("DEMO_KEY", raising=False)
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path) == {"a": "", "b": "fallback", "c": "x-y-z"}
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_KEY", "")  # set-but-empty counts as unset for ":-", like the shell
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path)["b"] == "fallback"
    monkeypatch.setenv("DEMO_KEY", "real")
    assert load_config("demo", config_dir=tmp_path) == {"a": "real", "b": "real", "c": "x-real-z"}
```

```python
def test_llm_yaml_holds_no_literal_credential(monkeypatch):
    raw = yaml.safe_load((CONFIG_DIR / "agent/llm.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))  # un-interpolated, as committed
    assert re.fullmatch(r"\$\{[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(:-[^}]*)?\}", raw["api_key"] or "${EMPTY:-}"), (
        "api_key must be an env reference like ${LLM_API_KEY:-}, never a literal secret"
    )
    monkeypatch.delenv("LLM_API_KEY", raising=False)
    assert load_config("agent/llm")["api_key"] == ""  # no secret required to load the config (local servers, CI)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_API_KEY", "from-env")
    assert load_config("agent/llm")["api_key"] == "from-env"
```

_Test that modifying a config file clears the LRU cache in _limits.py._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_config_cache_invalidation_on_file_change(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Test that modifying a config file clears the LRU cache in _limits.py."""
    (tmp_path / "test_config.yaml").write_text("test:\n  value: 10\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # First load
    cfg1 = load_config("test_config", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg1["test"]["value"] == 10

    # Wait a bit to ensure mtime changes
    time.sleep(0.01)

    # Modify the file
    (tmp_path / "test_config.yaml").write_text("test:\n  value: 20\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # Second load should get the new value
    cfg2 = load_config("test_config", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg2["test"]["value"] == 20
```

```python
def test_config_schema_validation(tmp_path):
    """Test that Pydantic schema validation works for agent configs."""
    # Create agent subdirectory
    agent_dir = tmp_path / "agent"
    agent_dir.mkdir()

    # Valid config
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: 8\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    cfg = load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path)
    assert cfg["agent"]["max_steps"] == 8

    # Invalid config (negative value)
    (agent_dir / "agent.yaml").write_text(
        "agent:\n  max_steps: -1\n  plan_retries: 1\n  reflect_rounds: 1\n  reflect_error_chars: 300\n"
        "  max_tokens: 2048\n  temperature: 0.0\n  type1:\n    candidate_multiplier: 5\n    min_candidates: 50\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="Config validation failed"):
        load_config("agent/agent", config_dir=tmp_path)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_data_paths_layout_is_under_resource():
    paths = get_data_paths()
    assert paths.tag == "multi_domain"
    assert paths.raw_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "raw"
    assert paths.review_path("Video_Games").is_relative_to(paths.raw_dir)
    # meta_path has fallback logic: checks resource/metadata/ first, then resource/raw/meta_*.jsonl, then resource/raw/meta_*.jsonl.gz
    # Since resource/metadata/meta_Video_Games.jsonl doesn't exist, it falls back to resource/raw/meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz
    assert paths.meta_path("Video_Games") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource" / "raw" / "meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz"
    assert paths.cleaned_path("Electronics") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/cleaned/Electronics/interactions.parquet"
    assert paths.filtered_path == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/filtered/multi_domain/interactions.parquet"
    assert paths.mapped_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/mapped/multi_domain"
    assert paths.splits_dir == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/splits/multi_domain"
    assert paths.split_path("train") == PROJECT_ROOT / "resource/splits/multi_domain/train.parquet"
```

```python
def test_logs_and_experiments_are_separate_semantic_dirs():
```

```python
def test_config_cli_prints_values_for_shell_scripts():
    def run(*args):
        out = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "package.config", *args], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=PROJECT_ROOT
        )
        return out.returncode, out.stdout.split()

    assert run("domains.domains") == (0, get_domains())
    assert run("run_tag.tag") == (0, [load_config("path/run_tag")["tag"]])
    assert run("data_paths.paths.raw_dir") == (0, ["resource/raw"])
    assert run("data_paths.does_not_exist")[0] != 0
```

```python
def test_config_cli_resolves_paths_from_datapaths_for_shell_scripts():
    def run(*args):
        out = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "package.config", *args], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=PROJECT_ROOT
        )
        return out.returncode, out.stdout.split()

    assert run("path:raw_dir") == (0, ["resource/raw"])
    assert run("path:filtered_path") == (0, ["resource/filtered/multi_domain/interactions.parquet"])
    assert run("--tag", "vg_toys", "path:splits_dir") == (0, ["resource/splits/vg_toys"])
    assert run("--tag", "vg_toys", "path:log_dir") == (0, ["experiments/logs/vg_toys"])
    assert run("path:experiments_dir") == (0, ["experiments"])
    assert run("path:review_path:Video_Games") == (0, [get_data_paths().review_path("Video_Games").relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()])
    assert run("path:meta_path:Video_Games") == (0, ["resource/raw/meta_Video_Games.jsonl.gz"])
    assert run("path:cleaned_path:Electronics") == (0, ["resource/cleaned/Electronics/interactions.parquet"])
    assert run("--tag", "t", "path:split_path:train") == (0, ["resource/splits/t/train.parquet"])
    # shell and Python must agree: the CLI is just a view of DataPaths
    assert run("path:split_path:test") == (0, [get_data_paths().split_path("test").relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()])
    assert run("path:nope")[0] != 0
    assert run("path:cleaned_path")[0] != 0  # needs an argument
    assert run("path:raw_dir:oops")[0] != 0  # takes none
    assert run("path:split_path:validation_typo")[0] != 0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_data_paths.py::test_review_resolver_supports_actual_layout_and_legacy_fallback`  (0.0076s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_review_resolver_supports_actual_layout_and_legacy_fallback(tmp_path):
    from dataclasses import replace
    paths = replace(get_data_paths(), raw_dir=tmp_path)
    assert paths.review_path("D") == tmp_path / "D.jsonl.gz"
    legacy = tmp_path / "D.jsonl.gz"
    legacy.write_bytes(b"test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == legacy
    plain = tmp_path / "D.jsonl"
    plain.write_text("test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == plain
    nested = tmp_path / "D" / "D.jsonl"
    nested.parent.mkdir()
    nested.write_text("test")
    assert paths.review_path("D") == nested
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_dependency_direction():
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        owner = _owner_package(_dotted_module(path))
        if owner is None:
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        for dotted, lineno in _imported_modules(path):
            for forbidden in FORBIDDEN_IMPORTS[owner]:
                if _is_under(dotted, forbidden) and not any(
                    rel == file and _is_under(dotted, allowed) for file, allowed in ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS
                ):
                    offenders.append(f"{rel}:{lineno}: {owner} must not import {dotted}")
    assert not offenders, "dependency-direction violations:\n" + "\n".join(sorted(set(offenders)))
```

```python
def test_allowed_exceptions_are_still_needed():
    """An exception that nothing uses any more must be deleted, so the list cannot rot."""
    used = set()
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        for dotted, _ in _imported_modules(path):
            used.update((rel, allowed) for file, allowed in ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS if rel == file and _is_under(dotted, allowed))
    assert used == ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_domain_registry.py::test_merge_domains_prevents_id_collision`  (0.0151s)

_Test QUAN TRỌNG NHẤT: cả 2 domain đều có raw parent_asin='ASIN_X'._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_merge_domains_prevents_id_collision(video_games_df, toys_games_df):
    """
    Test QUAN TRỌNG NHẤT: cả 2 domain đều có raw parent_asin='ASIN_X'.
    Nếu không namespace đúng, 2 item hoàn toàn khác nhau (1 video game,
    1 món đồ chơi) sẽ bị hệ thống hiểu nhầm là CÙNG 1 item.
    """
    tagged_vg = tag_domain(video_games_df, "Video_Games")
    tagged_tg = tag_domain(toys_games_df, "Toys_and_Games")
    merged = merge_domains([tagged_vg, tagged_tg])

    namespaced_ids = set(merged["parent_asin"])
    assert f"Video_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" in namespaced_ids
    assert f"Toys_and_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" in namespaced_ids
    # 2 item id namespaced phải KHÁC NHAU dù raw asin giống nhau
    assert f"Video_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X" != f"Toys_and_Games{ITEM_ID_SEPARATOR}ASIN_X"
    # tổng số dòng đúng bằng tổng 2 domain (không mất/nhân đôi dữ liệu)
    assert len(merged) == len(video_games_df) + len(toys_games_df)
```

```python
def test_merge_domains_preserves_shared_user_id_across_domains(video_games_df, toys_games_df):
    """
    u1 xuất hiện ở CẢ 2 domain — đây chính là cơ chế giảm cold-start
    (D-010). Verify user_id không bị namespace/đổi, để hệ thống nhận ra
    đây là CÙNG 1 user.

    tagged_tg = tag_domain(toys_games_df, "Toys_and_Games")

    u1_rows = merged[merged["user_id"] == "u1"]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/config/test_import_resolution.py::test_all_package_imports_have_existing_files`  (1.3273s)

_Ensure all package.* imports in package/, scripts/, tests/ point to existing files._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_all_package_imports_have_existing_files():
    """Ensure all package.* imports in package/, scripts/, tests/ point to existing files."""
    offenders = []

    for path in _py_files():
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            imports = _extract_imports(tree)

            for module_name, lineno in imports:
                # Only check package.* imports
                if module_name.startswith("package."):
                    if not _module_file_exists(module_name):
                        rel_path = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)
                        offenders.append(f"{rel_path}:{lineno}: from {module_name}")
        except SyntaxError:
            # Skip files with syntax errors (they'll be caught by other tests)
            continue

    assert not offenders, "Imports to non-existent package modules:\n" + "\n".join(offenders)
```

```python
def test_every_public_module_has_one_public_symbol_named_after_the_file():
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        if path.stem.startswith("_") or path.stem in SKIPPED_STEMS:
            continue
        symbols = _public_top_level(path)
        rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
        if len(symbols) != 1:
            offenders.append(f"{rel}: expected exactly 1 public class/function, found {symbols or 'none'}")
        elif _norm(symbols[0]) != _norm(path.stem):
            offenders.append(f"{rel}: public symbol {symbols[0]!r} does not match the file name")
    assert not offenders, "atomic-file / name-match violations:\n" + "\n".join(offenders)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_public_names_are_unique_across_the_package():
    seen: dict[str, str] = {}
    duplicates = []
    for path in _py_files(PACKAGE_ROOT):
        if path.stem.startswith("_") or path.stem in SKIPPED_STEMS:
            continue
        for name in _public_top_level(path):
            rel = path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()
            if name in seen:
                duplicates.append(f"{name}: {seen[name]} and {rel}")
            seen.setdefault(name, rel)
    assert not duplicates, "duplicate public names:\n" + "\n".join(duplicates)
```

```python
def test_no_module_imports_a_private_module_of_another_folder():
    offenders = []
    for root in CHECKED_ROOTS:
        for path in _py_files(root):
            importer = _dotted_module(path)
            for dotted, lineno in _imported_modules(path):
                if not dotted.startswith("package.") or not _module_exists(dotted):
                    continue
                owner = _private_owner(dotted)
                if owner is None:
                    continue
                inside_owner = importer == owner or importer.startswith(owner + ".")
                if not inside_owner:
                    offenders.append(f"{path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix()}:{lineno}: imports {dotted}")
    assert not offenders, "cross-folder imports of `_` modules:\n" + "\n".join(sorted(set(offenders)))
```

```python
def test_no_hardcoded_project_dirs_in_code():
    # Directories come from DataPaths (configs/path/data_paths.yaml). Docstrings/comments may mention them.
    offenders = []
    for path in _py_files():
        for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, str):
                v = node.value
                if v == "experiments" or v.startswith(("experiments/", "resource/")) or v == "resource":
                    offenders.append(f"{path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)}:{node.lineno}: {v!r}")
    assert not offenders, "hard-coded project paths:\n" + "\n".join(offenders)
```
